class op7_loop_body:
    var_ranges = {p0: 2, p1: 512}
    index0 = 512*p0 + p1
    index1 = p1
    index2 = p1 + 512
    def body(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index0')
        load = ops.load('le', get_index)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index0')
        load_1 = ops.load('buf0', get_index_1)
        constant = ops.constant(1.0, torch.float32)
        truediv = ops.truediv(load_1, constant)
        constant_1 = ops.constant(0.0, torch.float32)
        where = ops.where(load, constant_1, truediv)
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index0')
        load_2 = ops.load('convolution_19', get_index_2)
        get_index_3 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_3 = ops.load('buf3', get_index_3)
        sub = ops.sub(load_2, load_3)
        get_index_4 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_4 = ops.load('buf5', get_index_4)
        constant_2 = ops.constant(0.5, torch.float32)
        mul = ops.mul(load_4, constant_2)
        get_index_5 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_5 = ops.load('convolution_19', get_index_5)
        get_index_6 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_6 = ops.load('buf3', get_index_6)
        sub_1 = ops.sub(load_5, load_6)
        square = ops.square(sub_1)
        get_index_7 = self.get_index('index2')
        load_7 = ops.load('convolution_19', get_index_7)
        get_index_8 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_8 = ops.load('buf3', get_index_8)
        sub_2 = ops.sub(load_7, load_8)
        square_1 = ops.square(sub_2)
        add = ops.add(square, square_1)
        constant_3 = ops.constant(2.0, torch.float32)
        truediv_1 = ops.truediv(add, constant_3)
        constant_4 = ops.constant(1e-05, torch.float32)
        add_1 = ops.add(truediv_1, constant_4)
        rsqrt = ops.rsqrt(add_1)
        get_index_9 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_9 = ops.load('convolution_19', get_index_9)
        get_index_10 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_10 = ops.load('buf3', get_index_10)

class op49_loop_body:
    var_ranges = {p0: 8, p1: 256}
    index0 = 256*p0 + p1
    index1 = p1
    def body(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index0')
        load = ops.load('relu_12', get_index)
        constant = ops.constant(0.0, torch.float32)
        le = ops.le(load, constant)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index0')
        load_1 = ops.load('buf25', get_index_1)
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index0')
        load_2 = ops.load('buf41', get_index_2)
        add = ops.add(load_1, load_2)
        constant_1 = ops.constant(0.0, torch.float32)
        where = ops.where(le, constant_1, add)
        get_index_3 = self.get_index('index0')
        load_3 = ops.load('convolution_14', get_index_3)
        get_index_4 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_4 = ops.load('buf44', get_index_4)
        sub = ops.sub(load_3, load_4)
        get_index_5 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_5 = ops.load('buf47', get_index_5)
        constant_2 = ops.constant(0.125, torch.float32)
        mul = ops.mul(load_5, constant_2)
        get_index_6 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_6 = ops.load('buf45', get_index_6)
        constant_3 = ops.constant(8.0, torch.float32)
        truediv = ops.truediv(load_6, constant_3)
        constant_4 = ops.constant(1e-05, torch.float32)
        add_1 = ops.add(truediv, constant_4)
        rsqrt = ops.rsqrt(add_1)
        get_index_7 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_7 = ops.load('buf45', get_index_7)
        constant_5 = ops.constant(8.0, torch.float32)
        truediv_1 = ops.truediv(load_7, constant_5)
        constant_6 = ops.constant(1e-05, torch.float32)
        add_2 = ops.add(truediv_1, constant_6)
        rsqrt_1 = ops.rsqrt(add_2)
        mul_1 = ops.mul(rsqrt, rsqrt_1)
        mul_2 = ops.mul(mul, mul_1)
        mul_3 = ops.mul(sub, mul_2)
        sub_1 = ops.sub(where, mul_3)
        get_index_8 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_8 = ops.load('buf46', get_index_8)
        constant_7 = ops.constant(0.125, torch.float32)
        mul_4 = ops.mul(load_8, constant_7)
        sub_2 = ops.sub(sub_1, mul_4)
        get_index_9 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_9 = ops.load('buf45', get_index_9)
        constant_8 = ops.constant(8.0, torch.float32)
        truediv_2 = ops.truediv(load_9, constant_8)
        constant_9 = ops.constant(1e-05, torch.float32)
        add_3 = ops.add(truediv_2, constant_9)
        rsqrt_2 = ops.rsqrt(add_3)
        get_index_10 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_10 = ops.load('primals_90', get_index_10)
        mul_5 = ops.mul(rsqrt_2, load_10)
        mul_6 = ops.mul(sub_2, mul_5)
        get_index_11 = self.get_index('index0')
        store = ops.store('buf49', get_index_11, mul_6, None)
        return store

    index1 = p1
    def body(self, ops):

        le = ops.le(load, constant)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index0')
        load_1 = ops.load('buf121', get_index_1)
        constant_1 = ops.constant(0.0, torch.float32)
        where = ops.where(le, constant_1, load_1)
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index0')
        load_2 = ops.load('convolution_5', get_index_2)
        get_index_3 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_3 = ops.load('unsqueeze_250', get_index_3)
        sub = ops.sub(load_2, load_3)
        get_index_4 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_4 = ops.load('buf124', get_index_4)
        constant_2 = ops.constant(0.03125, torch.float32)
        mul = ops.mul(load_4, constant_2)
        get_index_5 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_5 = ops.load('squeeze_16', get_index_5)
        get_index_6 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_6 = ops.load('squeeze_16', get_index_6)
        mul_1 = ops.mul(load_5, load_6)
        mul_2 = ops.mul(mul, mul_1)
        mul_3 = ops.mul(sub, mul_2)
        sub_1 = ops.sub(where, mul_3)
        get_index_7 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_7 = ops.load('buf123', get_index_7)
        constant_3 = ops.constant(0.03125, torch.float32)
        mul_4 = ops.mul(load_7, constant_3)
        sub_2 = ops.sub(sub_1, mul_4)
        get_index_8 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_8 = ops.load('squeeze_16', get_index_8)
        get_index_9 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_9 = ops.load('primals_36', get_index_9)
        mul_5 = ops.mul(load_8, load_9)
        mul_6 = ops.mul(sub_2, mul_5)
        get_index_10 = self.get_index('index0')
        store = ops.store('buf126', get_index_10, mul_6, None)
        return store

        return store
